  - D1-c loss(e) = qty*(p_recon - p_exec) + (catch-up slippage + catch-up fee), qty signed (v0.4 sign fix vs v0.3, which had the first term negated). loss(e) can be negative (a missed trade followed by a favorable price move before catch-up is a net benefit). Does not add back the fee/slippage the original (now-skipped) t+δ trade would have paid — the formula as given only adds the catch-up leg's own friction, so loss(e) is the INCREMENTAL cost of deferring this one trade, not a full swap-accounting of both legs.
